20736:   function ocMarkup(events){

20738:     const groups=new Map();
20739:     events.forEach(event=>{
20740:       const key=event.oc||'Sin OC individual';
20741:       if(!groups.has(key))groups.set(key,[]);

20743:     });

20682:   function timelineDateToken(value){
20683:     const raw=text(value);
20684:     if(validDate(raw))return raw;
20685:     const match=raw.match(/^(\d{1,2})[\/\-](\d{1,2})[\/\-](\d{4})$/);
20686:     if(!match)return '';
20687:     const iso=`${match[3]}-${String(match[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(match[1]).padStart(2,'0')}`;
20688:     return validDate(iso);
20689:   }

20727:   function dailyMarkup(events){
20728:     if(!events.length)return '<div class="timeline-empty">No hay movimientos para los filtros seleccionados.</div>';
20729:     const groups=new Map();
20730:     events.forEach(event=>{
20731:       if(!groups.has(event.fecha))groups.set(event.fecha,[]);
20732:       groups.get(event.fecha).push(event);
20733:     });
20734:     return [...groups.entries()].map(([date,list])=>`<section class="timeline-date-group"><div class="timeline-section-head"><div><h3>${html(displayDate(date))}</h3><p>${list.length} movimiento(s)</p></div></div>${list.map(event=>eventCard(event,false)).join('')}</section>`).join('');
20735:   }
20736:   function ocMarkup(events){
20737:     if(!events.length)return '<div class="timeline-empty">No hay eventos vinculados a la OC indicada.</div>';
20738:     const groups=new Map();
20739:     events.forEach(event=>{
20740:       const key=event.oc||'Sin OC individual';
20741:       if(!groups.has(key))groups.set(key,[]);
20742:       groups.get(key).push(event);
20743:     });
20744:     return [...groups.entries()].map(([oc,list])=>{
20745:       const ordered=sortEvents(list);
20746:       const action=nextAction(ordered);
20747:       return `<section class="timeline-oc-group"><div class="timeline-section-head"><div><h3>${oc==='Sin OC individual'?html(oc):`OC ${html(oc)}`}</h3><p>${ordered.length} movimiento(s) \u00b7 \u00daltimo: ${html(displayDate(ordered[0].fecha))}${action?` \u00b7 Pr\u00f3xima acci\u00f3n: ${html(action.accion_pendiente)}`:''}</p></div>${oc!=='Sin OC individual'?`<button type="button" data-timeline-open-oc="${attr(oc)}">Abrir ficha OC</button>`:''}</div><div class="timeline-vertical">${ordered.map(event=>eventCard(event,false)).join('')}</div></section>`;
20748:     }).join('');
20749:   }

20171:     }catch(error){

20173:       return [];
20174:     }

20679:   function isMailEvent(event){
20680:     return fold(event?.tipo_evento)==='mailing'||fold(event?.origen)==='mailing';
20681:   }
20682:   function timelineDateToken(value){
20683:     const raw=text(value);
20684:     if(validDate(raw))return raw;
20685:     const match=raw.match(/^(\d{1,2})[\/\-](\d{1,2})[\/\-](\d{4})$/);
20686:     if(!match)return '';
20687:     const iso=`${match[3]}-${String(match[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(match[1]).padStart(2,'0')}`;
20688:     return validDate(iso);
20689:   }
20690:   function isControlTercerosEvent(event){
20691:     if(!text(event?.oc))return false;
20692:     const source=fold([event?.origen,event?.tipo_evento,event?.titulo,event?.remitente,event?.descripcion,event?.observaciones].map(text).join(' '));
20693:     return fold(event?.origen)==='control de terceros'||source.includes('control de terceros')||source.includes('control de 3ros')||source.includes('control de 3eros');
20694:   }
20695:   function controlTercerosDateCandidate(event){
20696:     if(!isControlTercerosEvent(event))return '';
20697:     const structured=timelineDateToken(event?.fecha_limite);
20698:     if(structured)return structured;
20699:     const source=[event?.titulo,event?.descripcion,event?.observaciones,event?.accion_pendiente,event?.documentos_mencionados].map(text).filter(Boolean).join(' ');
20700:     const match=source.match(/(?:vigencia\s+)?(?:hasta(?:\s+el)?|vigente\s+hasta(?:\s+el)?|v[áa]lid[oa]\s+hasta(?:\s+el)?|vence(?:\s+el)?|vencimiento(?:\s+el)?)\s*[:\-]?\s*(\d{1,2}[\/\-]\d{1,2}[\/\-]\d{4}|\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})/i);
20701:     return timelineDateToken(match?.[1]||'');
20702:   }

20918:   function timelineBulkCounts(events){
20919:     const list=Array.isArray(events)?events:[];
20920:     return {
20921:       detected:list.length,
20922:       valid:list.filter(event=>event._bulk?.valid&&!event._bulk?.duplicate).length,
20923:       observed:list.filter(event=>event._bulk?.valid&&event._bulk?.validation?.length&&!event._bulk?.duplicate).length,
20924:       duplicated:list.filter(event=>event._bulk?.duplicate).length,
20925:       error:list.filter(event=>!event._bulk?.valid).length
20926:     };
20927:   }

20897:       const tipo_evento=normalizeTimelineEnum(rawObject.tipo_evento,TIMELINE_EVENT_TYPES,'Mailing');
20898:       const event=normalizeEvent({
20899:         ...rawObject,
20900:         id:makeId(),fecha,hora,semana:isoWeek(fecha),estado,riesgo,tipo_evento,
20901:         origen:text(rawObject.origen)||'Mailing',fecha_limite:rawObject.fecha_limite?normalizeTimelineDate(rawObject.fecha_limite):'',
20902:         titulo:text(rawObject.titulo)||`${tipo_evento}${rawObject.oc?' OC '+rawObject.oc:''}`,
20903:         creado_por:userName(),actualizado_en:nowISO(),origen_carga:'Carga masiva ChatGPT / Excel'
20904:       },index);
20905:       if(rawObject.estado&&estado!==rawObject.estado)validation.push('Estado normalizado');
20906:       if(rawObject.riesgo&&riesgo!==rawObject.riesgo)validation.push('Riesgo normalizado');
20907:       if(rawObject.tipo_evento&&tipo_evento!==rawObject.tipo_evento)validation.push('Tipo normalizado');
20908:       completeEventFromOrder(event,validation);
20909:       if(!text(event.titulo))validation.push('Título vacío');
20910:       const key=timelineDuplicateKey(event);
20911:       const duplicated=existingKeys.has(key)||previewKeys.has(key);
20912:       if(duplicated)validation.push('Posible duplicado');
20913:       previewKeys.add(key);
20914:       event._bulk={row:index+1,valid:!!text(event.titulo),duplicate:duplicated,validation};
20915:       return event;
20916:     });
20917:   }

20943:   async function importTimelineEvents(events){

20950:       state.bulk.recent=clean.map(event=>({...event})).slice(0,12);
20951:       state.bulk.lastMessage=`${result.count} evento(s) guardado(s) at\u00f3micamente en Supabase.${result.refreshed?'':' La lista completa requiere una actualizaci\u00f3n manual.'}${allow?' Se permitieron duplicados seleccionados.':''}`;
20952:       renderTimelineCOI();
20953:       if(typeof window.mostrarMensajeCOI==='function')window.mostrarMensajeCOI(`Timeline guardado en Supabase: ${result.count} evento(s).`,'ok');

21205:   function wrapRender(name,after,marker){
21206:     const base=window[name];
21207:     if(typeof base!=='function'||base[marker])return;
21208:     const wrapped=function(){
21209:       const result=base.apply(this,arguments);
21210:       try{after.apply(this,arguments);}catch(error){console.warn(`Timeline COI: integraci\u00f3n ${name} no disponible`,error);}
21211:       if(result&&typeof result.then==='function')result.finally(()=>{try{after.apply(this,arguments);}catch(error){}});
21212:       return result;
21213:     };
21214:     wrapped[marker]=true;
21215:     wrapped.__timelineBase=base;
21216:     window[name]=wrapped;
21217:   }

13170:   function parsearNumeroCertificacion(value){
13171:     if(typeof value==='number')return {valido:Number.isFinite(value),valor:Number.isFinite(value)?value:0};
13172:     const original=clean(value);
13173:     if(original===''||original==='-')return {valido:true,valor:0};
13174:     let raw=original.replace(/\s/g,'');
13175:     if(raw.includes(',')&&raw.includes('.')){
13176:       raw=raw.lastIndexOf(',')>raw.lastIndexOf('.')?raw.replace(/\./g,'').replace(',','.'):raw.replace(/,/g,'');
13177:     }else if(raw.includes(','))raw=raw.replace(',','.');
13178:     raw=raw.replace(/[$%]/g,'');
13179:     if(!/^-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(raw))return {valido:false,valor:0};
13180:     const number=Number(raw);
13181:     return {valido:Number.isFinite(number),valor:Number.isFinite(number)?number:0};
13182:   }

13192:   function normalizarFechaCertificacion(value){
13193:     if(value instanceof Date&&!Number.isNaN(value.getTime()))return value.toISOString().slice(0,10);
13194:     const raw=clean(value);
13195:     if(!raw)return '';
13196:     const validParts=(year,month,day)=>{
13197:       const date=new Date(Number(year),Number(month)-1,Number(day));
13198:       return date.getFullYear()===Number(year)&&date.getMonth()===Number(month)-1&&date.getDate()===Number(day);
13199:     };
13200:     let match=raw.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})(?:[T\s].*)?$/);
13201:     if(match&&validParts(match[1],match[2],match[3]))return `${match[1]}-${String(match[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(match[3]).padStart(2,'0')}`;
13202:     match=raw.match(/^(\d{1,2})[\/\-.](\d{1,2})[\/\-.](\d{2}|\d{4})$/);
13203:     if(match){
13204:       const year=match[3].length===2?`20${match[3]}`:match[3];
13205:       if(validParts(year,match[2],match[1]))return `${year}-${String(match[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(match[1]).padStart(2,'0')}`;
13206:     }
13207:     return '';
13208:   }

13332:   function detalleErrorEsquema(error){
13333:     const code=clean(error?.code);
13334:     const message=clean(error?.message||error?.details||error);
13335:     const details=clean(error?.details);
13336:     const hint=clean(error?.hint);
13337:     if(code==='PGRST205'||code==='42P01')return 'La tabla public.coi_certificaciones no está disponible para la API de Supabase.';
13338:     if(code==='PGRST204'||code==='42703')return `La tabla existe, pero falta una columna esperada: ${message}`;
13339:     if(code==='42501'||/row-level security|permission denied/i.test(message))return 'La sesión no tiene permisos para guardar certificaciones. Revise RLS y políticas para authenticated.';
13340:     if(code==='23505')return 'La certificación ya existe. Se debe actualizar el registro existente.';
13341:     if(code==='42P10'||/no unique or exclusion constraint/i.test(message))return 'Supabase no encontró un índice único compatible; se aplicará actualización o inserción controlada.';
13342:     if(code==='OC_NOT_FOUND')return message;
13343:     return [code?`${code}: ${message}`:message,details&&details!==message?details:'',hint?`Sugerencia: ${hint}`:''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')||'Supabase no pudo completar la operación.';
13344:   }

13346:   async function resolverOrdenCertificacionPorNroOC(nroOC,client){
13347:     const target=normalizarNroOCCertificacion(nroOC);
13348:     if(!target){const error=new Error('Falta OC.');error.code='OC_NOT_FOUND';throw error;}
13349:     const local=buscarOrdenPorNroOC(target);
13350:     if(local?.orden_id)return local;
13351:     const exact=await client.from(ORDENES_TABLE).select('id,nro_oc,id_obra,proveedor,tipo_trabajo,descripcion,estacion').eq('nro_oc',target).limit(1);
13352:     if(exact.error)throw crearErrorSupabase(exact.error,'buscar OC',{nro_oc:target});
13353:     let row=Array.isArray(exact.data)?exact.data[0]:null;
13354:     if(!row){
13355:       const fallback=await client.from(ORDENES_TABLE).select('id,nro_oc,id_obra,proveedor,tipo_trabajo,descripcion,estacion').limit(5000);
13356:       if(fallback.error)throw crearErrorSupabase(fallback.error,'buscar OC normalizada',{nro_oc:target});
13357:       row=(fallback.data||[]).find(item=>normalizarNroOCCertificacion(item?.nro_oc)===target)||null;
13358:     }
13359:     if(!row){const error=new Error(`No se encontró la OC ${target} en public.coi_ordenes.`);error.code='OC_NOT_FOUND';throw error;}
13360:     return {
13361:       row,item:row,nro:target,orden_id:isUUID(row.id)?row.id:null,id_obra:clean(row.id_obra),

20271:   async function fetchTimelineEventsSupabase(client){
20272:     const rows=[];
20273:     let cursor=null;
20274:     for(;;){
20275:       const {data,error}=await client.rpc('coi_timeline_list_page',{
20276:         p_before_fecha:cursor?.fecha||null,
20277:         p_before_hora:cursor?.hora||null,
20278:         p_before_id:cursor?.id||null,
20279:         p_limit:TIMELINE_PAGE_SIZE
20280:       });
20281:       if(error)throw error;
20282:       const batch=Array.isArray(data)?data:[];
20283:       rows.push(...batch);
20284:       if(batch.length<TIMELINE_PAGE_SIZE)break;
20285:       const last=batch[batch.length-1];
20286:       cursor={fecha:last.fecha,hora:text(last.hora).slice(0,8),id:last.id};
20287:     }
20288:     return sortEvents(rows.map(databaseToTimelineEvent));
20289:   }
20290:   async function upsertTimelineEventsSupabase(client,events){
20291:     const list=Array.isArray(events)?events:[];
20292:     if(!list.length)return [];
20293:     if(list.length>5000)throw new Error('La operaci\u00f3n supera el l\u00edmite transaccional de 5.000 eventos.');
20294:     const payload=list.map(timelineEventToDatabase);
20295:     const {data,error}=await client.rpc('coi_timeline_upsert_events',{p_events:payload});
20296:     if(error)throw error;
20297:     return (data||[]).map(databaseToTimelineEvent);
20298:   }
20299:   async function migrateLegacyTimeline(client,remoteEvents){
20300:     if(localStorage.getItem(TIMELINE_MIGRATION_KEY))return {migrated:0};
20301:     const remoteIds=new Set((remoteEvents||[]).map(event=>event.id));
20302:     const legacy=readTimelineLegacy().filter(event=>event.id&&!event.id.startsWith('TL-DEMO-')&&text(event.titulo));
20303:     const pending=legacy.filter(event=>!remoteIds.has(event.id));
20304:     if(pending.length)await upsertTimelineEventsSupabase(client,pending);
20305:     try{localStorage.removeItem(TIMELINE_LEGACY_KEY);}catch(error){console.warn('Timeline COI: Supabase confirmó la migración, pero no se pudo retirar la entrada legacy.',error);}
20306:     try{localStorage.setItem(TIMELINE_MIGRATION_KEY,JSON.stringify({completedAt:nowISO(),migrated:pending.length}));}
20307:     catch(error){console.warn('Timeline COI: la migración quedó confirmada en Supabase; el marcador local es no autoritativo.',error);}
20308:     return {migrated:pending.length};
20309:   }

20869:   function parseTimelineBulkText(textValue){
20870:     const raw=textValue??'';
20871:     const lines=String(raw).replace(/\r/g,'').split('\n').filter(line=>line.trim().length);
20872:     if(!lines.length)return [];
20873:     const first=lines[0];
20874:     const delimiter=(first.match(/\t/g)||[]).length>0?'\t':';';
20875:     let headers=TIMELINE_BULK_COLUMNS.slice();
20876:     let start=0;
20877:     const firstCells=splitTimelineBulkLine(first,delimiter).map(cell=>text(cell).replace(/^"|"$/g,''));
20878:     if(isBulkHeader(firstCells)){
20879:       headers=firstCells.map(normalizeBulkHeaderName);
20880:       start=1;
20881:     }
20882:     const existingKeys=new Set(window.coiTimelineEvents.map(timelineDuplicateKey));
20883:     const previewKeys=new Set();
20884:     return lines.slice(start).map((line,index)=>{
20885:       const cells=splitTimelineBulkLine(line,delimiter);
20886:       const rawObject={};
20887:       headers.forEach((header,idx)=>{
20888:         const normalized=normalizeBulkHeaderName(header);
20889:         const field=TIMELINE_BULK_FIELD_MAP[normalized]||TIMELINE_BULK_FIELD_MAP[TIMELINE_BULK_COLUMNS[idx]];
20890:         if(field)rawObject[field]=text(cells[idx]??'');
20891:       });
20892:       const validation=[];
20893:       const fecha=normalizeTimelineDate(rawObject.fecha);
20894:       const hora=normalizeTimelineTime(rawObject.hora);
20895:       const estado=normalizeTimelineEnum(rawObject.estado,TIMELINE_STATUSES,'Informativo');
20896:       const riesgo=normalizeTimelineEnum(rawObject.riesgo,TIMELINE_RISKS,'Bajo');
20897:       const tipo_evento=normalizeTimelineEnum(rawObject.tipo_evento,TIMELINE_EVENT_TYPES,'Mailing');
20898:       const event=normalizeEvent({
20899:         ...rawObject,
20900:         id:makeId(),fecha,hora,semana:isoWeek(fecha),estado,riesgo,tipo_evento,
20901:         origen:text(rawObject.origen)||'Mailing',fecha_limite:rawObject.fecha_limite?normalizeTimelineDate(rawObject.fecha_limite):'',
